Runtime value is 1 and a restart defaults back to 1, so behavior is correct either way. `down-after-milliseconds` and `failover-timeout` differ from defaults and are written explicitly.\n\nI also round-tripped a key through the master to confirm it reached both replicas, deleted it, then `BGSAVE`d all three so each distinct RDB file exists on disk (173 bytes each).\n\n`/results/sentinel.json` is ASCII (valid UTF-8) with a trailing newline, parses cleanly, and has the ports sorted ascending:\n\n```json\n{\n  \"master_name\": \"shop-master\",\n  \"master_addr\": [\"127.0.0.1\", \"6379\"],\n  \"num_other_sentinels\": 2,\n  \"num_slaves\": 2,\n  \"sentinels\": [26379, 26380, 26381]\n}\n```\n\nThe cell has no authentication, as you specified — it's reachable only over loopback, so nothing is network-exposed, but anything with local access to this container has full admin on all six ports.","ttft_ms":2846,"type":"result","duration_ms":409066,"uuid":"f3c17074-5531-43b5-a58e-36ec55d0f95f","ttft_stream_ms":2272,"time_to_request_ms":52,"first_content_frame_ms":2360,"queued_turn_count":0,"result_index":0}
